===== issue body =====
## Problem

Replay artifacts report judge outcomes through `tally`, `decisive_margin`, and per-task `rows`. Promotion, regression, and leaderboard tooling consume those fields, but nothing verifies they agree. A hand-edited artifact could inflate `decisive_margin` while per-task rows tell a different story.

## Proposal

Add `benchmark/tally_integrity.py` and `scripts/tally_integrity.py` that verify, for each scored replay slice:

- `tally` carries numeric challenger/baseline/tie counts that sum to `tasks`
- when `rows` are present, `len(rows) == tasks` and winner labels recount to the same tally
- `decisive_margin` equals `challenger - baseline` when present

Support single-repo, multi-repo `per_repo` entries, and `--generalization` partitions. Expose a `--strict` CLI exit code for CI gating.

## Acceptance

- Offline unit tests under `tests/test_tally_integrity.py`
- `ruff check .` and `VANGUARSTEW_OFFLINE=1 python -m pytest -q` pass

./benchmark/promotion.py:15:   agent that does not actually out-decide the reference does not pass;

./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:12:change**. Per the blog: *the revealed git history is the spec, and `score.py` verifies against

./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:19:reproducible and resistant to prose-fluff, because it keys off real changed file paths and
./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:20:real release/commit facts — not free-text similarity. That contract must be explicit so agent
./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:21:devs know exactly what earns credit and reviewers can check scoring changes against it.

./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:27:   prose and is reproducible across runs.
./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:28:2. **As an agent developer**, I know precisely what the anchor rewards (naming the modules that
./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:29:   changed, predicting a release/bump when one happened, anticipating commit kinds) — so I
./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:31:3. **As a reviewer**, any change to `score.py` is checked against this written contract at the
./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:41:- The lexical `trajectory_overlap` SHALL remain a diagnostic only and SHALL NOT feed the score.

./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:53:  release wording or a version-leading subject) — an incidental version mention SHALL NOT count.
./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:56:  WHEN no release was signaled it SHALL NOT (so "predicted nothing" isn't trivially rewarded).
./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:65:  subjects) the plan's item kinds anticipated.
./specs/002-scoring-anchor/spec.md:67:  anticipation, but SHALL NOT feed the scalar `objective_component` (ranking-relevant fields
